FROM (
    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[Id], [u].[CreatedAt], [u].[CreatedBy], [u].[DeletedAt], [u].[DeletedBy], [u].[FailedLoginAttempts], [u].[IsActive], [u].[IsDeleted], [u].[IsResetPasswordCompleted], [u].[LockoutEnd], [u].[PendingTFATokenHash], [u].[ResetPasswordTokenExpiresAt], [u].[ResetPasswordTokenHash], [u].[TFACodeHash], [u].[TFAExpiresDate], [u].[TFAIsCompleted], [u].[TFAStatus], [u].[UpdatedAt], [u].[UpdatedBy], [u].[Id] AS [Id0], [u].[Email], [u].[Id] AS [Id1], [u].[FirstName], [u].[Id] AS [Id2], [u].[FullName], [u].[Id] AS [Id3], [u].[LastName], [u].[Id] AS [Id4], [u].[PasswordHash], [u].[Password_PasswordSalt], [u].[Id] AS [Id5], [u].[UserName]

    WHERE [u].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit) AND ([u].[Email] = @__loginIdentifier_0 OR [u].[UserName] = @__loginIdentifier_0_1)
) AS [u1]

    FROM [UserRoles] AS [u0]
    INNER JOIN (
        SELECT [r].[Id], [r].[CreatedAt], [r].[CreatedBy], [r].[DeletedAt], [r].[DeletedBy], [r].[Description], [r].[IsActive], [r].[IsDeleted], [r].[IsSystem], [r].[Name], [r].[UpdatedAt], [r].[UpdatedBy]
        FROM [Roles] AS [r]
        WHERE [r].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [r0] ON [u0].[RoleId] = [r0].[Id]

        FROM [RolePermissions] AS [r1]
        INNER JOIN (
            SELECT [p].[Id], [p].[CreatedAt], [p].[CreatedBy], [p].[DeletedAt], [p].[DeletedBy], [p].[Description], [p].[IsActive], [p].[IsDeleted], [p].[Name], [p].[UpdatedAt], [p].[UpdatedBy]
            FROM [Permissions] AS [p]
            WHERE [p].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
        ) AS [p0] ON [r1].[PermissionId] = [p0].[Id]
        WHERE [r1].[IsDeleted] = CAST(0 AS bit)
    ) AS [s] ON [r0].[Id] = [s].[RoleId]
